The claim is meant per obligation and without exception, which is what two completeness lemmas are for: without them every refusal of a deletion or an insertion would refute a decision procedure and leave the `Prop` it decides with no refutation at all. Among the constructions: an inventory naming a table, one naming R-07-027a's refused reply object and one naming a class twice; five numberings, adding a submission-queue opcode, a fifth reply group, the three schedule transitions or a notification half, and one dropping a member; three trap surfaces the criterion refuses, one per clause it fixes; two groupings; a dispatcher reading its opcode out of memory; four return paths; five transfers; a refusal spending the core's slot; three grants; a notification counter and an undefined reset; a work-stealing rotation; a mixed-label group; and two dispatch steps, one sharing a file across the group and one clearing the predecessor's as it leaves.
  * **What a refutation does not establish is stated where an obligation quantifies a field away**, because the coincidence invites the stronger reading. `SeesItsOwnPendingOnly` quantifies the interrupt file away, so it admits the unswapped construction on the whole static arm, a machine sharing one file across the boundary included, and that is proved rather than left to be discovered; R-07-044's *hidden or shared* is refuted on the carrier that can express it, the file-per-member state, arm-independently. Fourteen obligations carry a hypothesis and thirteen quantify it; the fourteenth reads R-07-044's arm, which is why it alone carries an inhabitation witness naming a machine and why the observable beside it carries no hypothesis.
  * **Where the register leaves a decision the artifact leaves a parameter, and two questions it would otherwise have taken by fiat are reported instead.** The trap surface is a parameter with `AdmissibleTrapSurface` as the criterion the register fixes over it, two values admitted and three refused; `no_admissible_trap_surface_is_the_abi_cut` proves the ABI cut of every admissible value rather than of the one the file names. The numbering obligation is stated against R-07-031b's own criterion for what may be numbered rather than against the specification's body, and the grouping obligation against that entry's own four-way assignment, so both decide where a clause read off the specification's body would not. Every composition magnitude is a record field, and the only literals are the four counts the register closes outright.
  * **The two dissolved questions stay dissolved and the count of what remains is the artifact's.** The endpoint's queue and its blocked-partition state do not exist, R-07-029a leaving no partition in a kernel-held wait, so an endpoint holds readiness and not a queue; and the notification object's representation is not owed here, both halves being memory operations. What is open is **nine register questions and not one**: the badge width this cell used to name alone, and eight beside it, two of which this authoring opened by refusing to decide by fiat what its own first draft had decided.
  * Nine findings, every one a register gap reported rather than closed, at a through g and i and j of the artifact's own header; a tenth lettered entry records that every composition magnitude is a field and is not an open question. **Two of the nine are new, and each is a question this artifact's own first draft decided by fiat.**
    * **The badge width of the message transfer shape is unfixed.** R-07-031 fixes the transfer and R-15-007 the bit budget, and nothing joins them, so the transfer is stated over an arbitrary width.
    * **What a typed refusal's type enumerates is unfixed.** R-07-029a requires the refusal and names no cause set.
    * **Which of the closed five invocations may refuse is unfixed**, owed at R-07-029a or R-08-004c.
    * **The ABI numbers themselves are fixed nowhere.** R-07-031b closes the surface at five and numbers none of them; the artifact exhibits a second numbering over the same five that passes the same check, so the choice is exhibited rather than argued.
    * **What a re-offer costs and how many are admitted is unfixed**, owed at R-07-042 or R-11-010.
    * **Whether a notification word is per partition, per endpoint or per ring is unfixed**, owed at R-12-096 or R-08-032.
    * **Whether an endpoint's readiness is one bit or one per offering peer is unfixed**, owed at R-07-029.
    * **What carries the compositor's request for a focus rebinding, a rung selection or a suspension is unfixed, and so whether the three trap.** R-07-031b names the three as kernel-enacted at a major-frame boundary without saying what carries the request, so the trap surface is a parameter, the criterion that entry does fix is stated over it, two values are admitted and three refused, and the one result that would otherwise turn on the gap is proved of every admissible value. Owed at R-11-023.
    * **Which of create, derive and revoke each surviving object class has is unfixed.** R-07-027a states the lifecycle only negatively of the two tables, R-07-031a deletes the derivation invocation and R-07-031b's closed five carries no create, so the artifact states the distinction the entry does draw and exhibits a second map discharging both obligations while disagreeing at cells no obligation reads. Owed at R-07-027a or R-07-031b.
  * **The gate's green line means compiled, axiom-free, non-vacuous and enumerated, and it does not mean verified.** Nothing here executes on either emulator.
  * Net change: 4,496 lines in one file, which is new.
  * Exit evidence: **471 new constants** at the proof gate, every one closed under the global context with no `Admitted`, no `Axiom`, no top-level `Parameter`, no top-level `Compute` and no `Require`, so nothing beyond the prelude is reachable and every equality is stated pointwise or over a decidable boolean for that reason; a seeded population of **274 live mutants, none stillborn, all 274 killed** over the whole population with no narrowing, the two survivors an earlier run found closed by **adding** the reflexivity and separation statements the file already makes of its other equalities; every cited requirement id resolving against the register by hand, which no rule holds for a `.v` file; the three host gates green with all 97 mutants killed and the registry covered; and both type checkers clean. No register, coverage-matrix, absence-contract or crown-jewel edit, so the review gate is not re-entered and the co-read ledger is unmoved.
  * Landed: Tier A.

### M4.3 · Exercise capability lifecycle, IPC, and slot faults through Wasm

  * Three files. [Vectors.v](../tools/quickchick/Vectors.v) gains a nineteen-family block over [EndpointIPC.v](../proofs/EndpointIPC.v) walked at a machine of its own, [Properties.v](../tools/quickchick/Properties.v) gains twenty-seven randomized property sets over the same subject, and [IPCProbe.v](../tools/quickchick/IPCProbe.v) is the composition both halves read so the two answer one question. The probe machine is deliberately not the artifact's `demo`: two fields are moved off it on purpose, so every column is a definition evaluated where the artifact never evaluates it.
  * **The cell's verb is met at the execution level and not only at the prover level**, which is what the `Wasm-parallel` convention asks a landed item to say. [ipc_oracle.v](../tools/wasm-oracle/ipc_oracle.v) is the **first repository Gallina source staged into the M1.5 CertiCoq-to-Wasm loop** rather than a smoke program: 84 checks over this lane's decision procedures folded into one boolean, compiled through CertiRocq 0.9.1's erasure and Wasm backend and run under node, which prints `true`. Docker is absent on this host, so the opam route is the one that ran. A recipe sits beside the M1.5 one and no `run.py` command reaches either.
  * **What that buys is a differential on the compiled pipeline, and it is measured rather than assumed.** Twelve one-token mutations seeded into the artifact's own deciders are **twelve stillborn**: the prover refuses the file first, every time. So the oracle cannot report a decider defect the proof gate would not already report, and its 84 checks are not a regression net over the deciders. What it uniquely tests is the pipeline itself, kernel conversion against CertiRocq's erasure and Wasm backend on a real artifact, which nothing in this tree had tested before.
  * **A green line is only worth having if a red one is reachable, so both are measured.** The same source seeded one token wrong, widening one mask family past the single mask that is the frozen surface, emits a different module that prints `false` and exits non-zero, so the falsity is computed across the family by the Wasm rather than read off a constant. Two randomized property sets were likewise hand-seeded and fail at 9 and 5 draws, which is the evidence a passing run cannot give.
  * **A column no input can move independently is a clause reported held that nothing holds**, so that shape is refused throughout: a `leb` column true of all five members is dropped rather than printed, and the pending family carries no static-arm column, that arm's delivery being provably the specification's. Where a definition is printed beside its expected value the agreement is by conversion and the walk says so, the domain deciding the references rather than the pairs: the successor's pending row and the predecessor's differ on 20 of the 48 rows.
  * Net change: 2,209 lines across five files, two of them new.
  * Exit evidence: **1,503 generated vectors** answered by the Gallina front against 1,152 before, **31 property sets** green under coq-quickchick 2.2.0 in the `quickchick-9.1.1` switch, the Wasm oracle green at 84 checks and red on its seeded twin at exit 1, and the artifact's own seeded population **274 of 274 killed**. The proof gate never compiles any of these three files and no constant in them reaches R-05-163's assumption enumeration. No register, coverage-matrix, absence-contract or crown-jewel edit.
  * Landed: Tier A.

## M5 · Storage and objects

### M5.1 · Implement the L0 journal and L1 CoW B-tree in Gallina

  * The Gallina reference §7 owes for the two lowest storage layers is [JournalIndex.v](../proofs/JournalIndex.v), in the idiom the landed Gallina items set. **No inductive at all**, because §10 closes no enumeration this layer needs: a block is a finite index and a medium is a total map from it, and the record and node granule counts, the block count, the fanout, the height, the allocator, the current root, the retained roots, the plan, the key algebra and the message authenticator are fields. The only numerals in a specification-side definition are three boundary zeroes, each commented at its site and pinned by a conversion.
  * **The recovery discipline is a parameter and neither arm is normative.** No entry chooses between stopping at the first record that does not verify and stepping over it, so every L0 obligation is stated over an arbitrary discipline and both arms are exhibited, each proved to keep every obligation the other keeps. What separates them is two predicates that are not obligations, and the choice is machine-checked to be observable: from one crashed journal the two arms reach different stores.
  * **Copy-on-write soundness is stated of an arbitrary machine, medium and commit under the admission predicate**, which is what makes that predicate gate something. Before the plan completes the published root is the previous one; past it the walk down the newly published root terminates at whole nodes to the declared height, and the three conjuncts carry it: the publish is last, so the new root is a block the plan wrote; the plan writes only blank blocks, so nothing the old medium covered moved; every child of a newly written node is either another block the plan wrote or one already covered. The demo commit is an instance of that theorem and not a second proof of it.
  * **Four recovery obligations, three refuted by construction and the fourth derived.** A recovery that zeroes untouched blocks breaks R-16-005's untouched half; one that replays nothing breaks its committed half; one that reads past its own discipline's cut breaks the discipline half and, necessarily, the untouched half with it. Idempotence has no construction that breaks it alone, and the file says why rather than leaving the absence unexplained: it follows from the other two, and that is a theorem.
  * **The node occupancy ceiling is a property of the tree.** `node_fits` reads a node's keys, its children and R-10-022a's one tag per child; the ceiling is stated over an arbitrary builder, computed over the demo tree's own nodes and over the nodes the landed commit publishes, and refuted of a builder cutting one key past the fanout and of a medium carrying a node one key too wide. The chunking lemma beneath it is named for what it is.
  * **Freshness is surrendered for one asset class of three, and this file states no property of that one.** R-10-011's MUST NOT is scoped to the mutable user-data volume and gives its reason; R-10-013b says the split *is three-way and not two* and puts durable component state beside it, carried by a freshness epoch rather than by the mutable volume's root. So what this file states no freshness property of is the L1 root of that one class, which is a scope with a ground rather than the silence R-10-013i would find against. The third class is stated in [KeyspaceDomains.v](../proofs/KeyspaceDomains.v) and its counter is M3.2's, already shipped.
  * **The gate's green line means compiled, axiom-free, non-vacuous and enumerated, and it does not mean verified.** Nothing here is compiled, lowered, or run on either emulator, and no byte reaches a device: R-12-025's raw NAND, R-12-026's per-page code and R-12-029's patrol scrub are not modelled, and R-12-030 is why that is admissible.
  * Net change: 4,269 lines in one file, which is new.
  * Eight findings, every one a register gap reported rather than closed.
    * **R-10-002 states no property of a commit.** It is a four-layer roster and a prover-provenance criterion, so the multi-block atomicity obligation is owed at R-10-036, whose acceptance clause is the one saying no partially updated region is ever committed.
    * **No entry chooses a recovery discipline.** R-10-002 names the GoJournal lineage and states no recovery rule; R-10-001a states the enumerate-and-select rule of the root copies and not of the log. Stopping and skipping differ observably.
    * **No entry of §10 states a block-reuse rule.** R-10-010's refcounted extent sharing is the nearest thing and states no obligation over the allocator; R-10-021 leaves the allocator below the integrity line trusted for availability, and R-10-027 gives the subvolumes one free-space pool without saying who may take from it. R-08-007a states such a rule of capability revocation and reaches no disk block.
    * **No entry fixes an atomic write unit for the persistent medium.** R-15-181 and R-15-247b fix one for the main-memory array and R-12-026 a per-page code for NAND; R-10-001a speaks of a torn write costing a copy without saying at what unit a write is atomic.
    * **No entry says an L0 record is self-verifying.** R-10-001a says it of a root candidate, and a log that cannot tell a torn record from a whole one has no recovery point at all.
    * **No entry names what ends a transaction.** R-10-036 commits a checkpoint as a single L0 transaction and names no mechanism for the commit itself.
    * **The word fanout occurs in no entry**, and no entry fixes a node's minimum occupancy or whether an empty index is a legal tree.
    * **Every composition magnitude is a field**, the demo instantiating each with a witness value that carries no claim.
  * Exit evidence: the proof gate green with **390 constants from this file**, every one closed under the global context, with no `Require`, no `Compute` and no top-level axiom; the **whole** seeded population at **210 mutants over eight operators, 210 killed, none surviving and none stillborn**; every cited requirement id resolved against the register by hand and every backticked identifier against a declaration, which no rule holds for a `.v` file; the three host gates green with all 97 checker mutants killed and the registry covered; and both type checkers clean. No `run.py evidence` sweep is owed: it measures the model build, the property harness, the profile sweep, the corpus, the devicetree and the reference, and this item touches none of them.
  * Landed: Tier A.

### M5.2 · Compose L2 semantics and L3 confidentiality host-side

  * The Gallina reference §7 owes for the two layers above the index is [KeyspaceDomains.v](../proofs/KeyspaceDomains.v), and it is the first artifact here with a `Require` on a sibling. R-10-005b makes a secondary index an instantiation of the one parametric L1 index, so the key algebra is [JournalIndex.v](../proofs/JournalIndex.v)'s record discharged at this key type; and the same entry puts the object, the metadata and the index update in one L0 transaction, so the atomicity obligation is stated over that file's own records, scan, commits and cuts.
  * **Nothing this file declares shadows the sibling it Requires**, and the four index theorems it inherits are instantiated at this key algebra by a single `exact` apiece, so *verified once and instantiated per object class* is a conversion rather than a sentence and the assumption block audits the imported constants rather than local twins of them. That is a hazard a `Require` carries and no gate reads: each file compiles separately and audits its own block, so a shadowed name costs no constant and is invisible to every count.
  * **One closed inductive and no second, and the licence is named.** R-10-005c writes *ordered add/remove deltas* and *one rescan-required marker*, which names what a delta is and what overflow answers, so the delta type carries three constructors on that entry's words. R-10-005's typed key kinds get the opposite treatment for the opposite reason: that entry lists four things L2 represents without saying the list is complete, and R-10-005b names a fifth candidate beside them, so the kind count is a field of the composition record and a kind is a finite index below it, exactly as a supervised unit is an index below its roster count. A composition declaring five is exhibited, admitting a keyspace the four-kind one refuses.
  * **R-10-005's bound is stated as the entry states it.** *O(1) writable snapshots* fixes a class and not a constant, so the obligation reads a declared cost and never zero, and a snapshotter writing one bookkeeping entry is admitted rather than refuted. Three obligations stand apart, four snapshotters decide them differently, and the copying snapshot is refuted of *every* declared constant rather than of the demo's.
  * **The atomicity corollary bites at the cuts where a mismatch is possible.** The obligation is the disjunction the acceptance clause carries, that at every crash point either neither the object nor the index is observed updated or both are, over an arbitrary writer and an arbitrary cut, gated by a second property of the writer: the transaction commits in a surviving prefix only where every block it names is in that prefix. A writer that closes its transaction before the index record has landed is refuted of it, and the store a reader would be standing in is computed.
  * **Freshness is a three-way split and this file carries the storage side of the third class.** R-10-011 surrenders it for the mutable user-data volume alone; R-10-013b puts durable component state beside that, declared `Fresh` and carried by a freshness epoch; R-10-035 lands that class here. Four obligations follow: the epoch root reads the version of every declared region and of nothing else, a write is acknowledged at the seal and never at the data commit, an unsealed epoch loses its writes rather than presenting them as fresh, and a version that does not verify is refused rather than returned. Three constructions break one apiece, and the commit-acknowledging one is **M3.2's own commit-advancing twin seen from this side of the seam**. The counter and the crypto core stay where they are; [RotFirmware.v](../proofs/RotFirmware.v) is named and not `Require`d.
  * **Non-interference is a two-state agreement over an arbitrary observer, authority set and pair of volumes**, and the four sealing obligations are decided independently by five sealings, with the bare-digest one non-interferent and a cross-domain confirmation oracle all the same.
  * **The gate's green line means compiled, axiom-free, non-vacuous and enumerated, and it does not mean verified.**
  * **What is not done, against this cell's own word.** This cell says *host-side*, which is the word separating it from M5.3's *run on the emulator*. The statement half is delivered and the execution half is not: nothing here is compiled, extracted, lowered or run on any host or emulator, and the computed checks are decided inside the kernel by conversion. Delivering the other half is the route this cell's `Wasm-parallel` label points at, and M4.3 has since walked it for the kernel surface: a generator over the key, delta and volume types, an extraction of the decidable predicates, and a harness that runs them. The cell is not narrowed to what was done, and the artifact's header says the same.
  * Net change: 4,202 lines in one file, which is new.
  * Nine findings, every one a register gap reported rather than closed.
    * **R-10-005 does not close its list of typed key kinds.** It names four things L2 represents, and R-10-005b adds *typed attribute value* without saying whether an attribute is a fifth kind or a component of the four. Closing it is a register act; M4.2b is the precedent, R-07-027a having had to close the object inventory at three classes before authoring could proceed.
    * **Whether a stored extent's existence is observable to a reader holding no key for its domain is unstated.**
    * **Whether an extent's plaintext length is observable is unstated, and no entry bounds the leak.** This is the gap with the largest consequence of the nine: a file-length channel is the residual a disk-encryption design ordinarily books explicitly.
    * **Whether the confidentiality-domain label of a stored extent is observable is unstated.**
    * **Whether the rescan-required marker occupies a slot of the declared queue bound or stands beside it is unstated.**
    * **Whether a recorded right may be persisted into a key is unstated**, where R-10-005b forbids a capability and R-08-037a admits exactly one durable record that names rights and confers none.
    * **R-10-005c names no order over its deltas.**
    * **What a `Fresh` region's version is, and what unit a region is, are unstated.** R-10-013c computes one root over *the version of every `Fresh` region* without saying whether that version is a counter, a content digest, or R-10-005's own snapshot version, and R-10-035 does not fix a region's extent in the keyspace.
    * **Every composition magnitude is a field**, including the kind roster and the snapshot's declared cost.
  * Exit evidence: the proof gate green with **397 constants from this file**, every one closed under the global context, with one `Require` naming a sibling in `proofs/`, no `Compute` and no top-level axiom; the **whole** seeded population at **377 mutants over eight operators, 377 killed, none surviving and none stillborn**, one survivor of the previous run closed by adding the statement its absence exposed rather than by narrowing; every backticked identifier resolved against a declaration by hand; the three host gates green; and both type checkers clean.
  * Landed: Tier A.

## M6 · Userland spine

### M6.0a · Decide the control-plane compiler provenance

  * **Contained, and the fork is admitted on Inria's own terms.** M6.1 lowers the init system's control plane with Vélus as a build-time producer this repository never redistributes, so §4's reciprocal clause is not engaged by anything here; and where a fork of it *is* published, it is published under the same terms and conditions as the Agreement, which §4 expressly permits. SECOMP is the standing demonstration that a fork of an Inria-licensed verified compiler is a workable base as long as the licence is preserved. Both other arms stay open.
  * **What separates this call from M1.1a is the grant and not the terms.** Every span quoted below from `INRIA/velus`'s agreement appears verbatim in `upstream/SECOMP`'s `LICENSE` at the pin, at the same section numbers and under the same headings, the reciprocal distribution clause at §4 included, and both grants are "nontransferable" in the same words. No clause this repository has read distinguishes the two, `INRIA/velus` being unpinned and its own file unread here, so neither containment recovers what the other forfeits.
  * **The third fork of this kind on the plan, and a decision rather than an edit.** `INRIA/velus`'s own `LICENSE` is the **Inria Non-Commercial License Agreement for the Vélus verified Lustre compiler**, and Vélus incorporates a modified CompCert as a submodule of its own. Four clauses are operative: §2 grants a "revocable, nonexclusive, nontransferable, royalty-free and worldwide license ... to use the Software solely for educational, research, or evaluation purposes" and permits Derivative Works "solely for academic, non-commercial research endeavors"; §3 provides that "Any other use is commercial use", making the restriction a property of each act of use rather than of the project; §4 requires a fork to be distributed "under the same terms and conditions as in this License"; and §7 terminates the grant "immediately without notice" on non-compliance. The licensor and the form of instrument are M1.1a's under a **separate grant**, so terms obtained for CompCert do not extend here.
  * What the arm still forfeits: §3 and §7 together mean nothing built this way survives a change in what the project is for without a new grant. It is right for a proof-of-concept and is not a position a shipped product can hold.
  * The arms not taken. **Obtain terms** is a separate Inria agreement, priced as a second negotiation even if M1.1a's are obtained. **Author instead** writes the control plane directly in Gallina and forfeits Lustre's structural WCET, determinism and causality argument, which is the whole reason §8 assigns the control plane to it; it is the cheapest arm to reach for later, §8 already keeping a Gallina reference model of the same state machine as its host-side oracle, so it starts from an artifact rather than from nothing.
  * Exit evidence: the arm is named, the arms not taken are recorded with what each forfeits, and `INRIA/velus`'s own licence file is read and quoted rather than inferred from CompCert's lineage in [THIRD-PARTY.md](../THIRD-PARTY.md), which carries §2, §3, §4 and §7 verbatim.

### M6.0b · Author the typed IDL profile

  * The vehicle every §12 interface is stated in is [idl-profile.md](idl-profile.md), which **is** the one typed profile R-12-010 fixes rather than a view of it: normative for its own language, defective where it disagrees with the register about a VerifiedOS obligation, and governing where the two disagree about the profile. That is the split R-05-135a already draws one artifact over, and [typed-assembly-language.md](typed-assembly-language.md) is the structural precedent taken whole: numbered `IDL-` requirements, one `· Accept:` and one `· Trace:` apiece, and a header block saying what the document is not.
  * **The start-from is named and both readings were taken at the file rather than at the forge.** The type layer forks WIT, the interface-definition syntax of `WebAssembly/component-model`, whose root `LICENSE` is a one-line pointer to a stock `Apache-2.0` with no exception and an unfilled appendix holder; the tree carries exactly two licence files and no subdirectory carve-out, so the pointer's deferral reaches nothing. The wire-layer exemplar is Fuchsia's FIDL and Zircon channel, whose `LICENSE` is `BSD-2-Clause` under *Copyright 2019 The Fuchsia Authors*, taken twice on two independent renderings because a two-clause result contradicting the usual three-clause characterization is the inference-from-lineage R-18-001a forbids. Both are permissive and non-reciprocal, so **the arm this repository has taken three times does not engage**: there is no term to refuse and none to contain, and nothing of either upstream is copied, so the claim at the top of [THIRD-PARTY.md](../THIRD-PARTY.md) is untouched.
  * **Both wire layers are declined on technical grounds and not licensing ones, and the two grounds are different.** WIT's own wire format is the Canonical ABI, stated over Core WebAssembly linear memory and lifting through a module's own memory; Wasm is not an execution target anywhere in the system (**R-05-085**, with R-14-013 for the specification's §14), no compartment the profile's interfaces are stated over is a Wasm module, and authority moves out of band as session-table indices (R-12-006, R-12-007), so that ABI has no machine here to be stated over. The clause naming the platform's Wasm posture is narrowed to the profile's own compartments rather than to the image, because R-14-013a ships exactly one pure-interpreter engine as a library compartment with guest state inside the embedding's CHERI-bounded memory plan: a machine in the image, reached through the host functions its embedding's manifest names and never through an interface of this profile. FIDL's is declined the other way about: its bytes-plus-out-of-band-handles model is structurally what the §12 data plane is, so the discipline transfers and is what §2, §3.3 and §4 carry, while its encoder and decoder are unverified C++ where marshalling here is a Narcissus copy-once verified parser generated from the type source. That the mapping is authored and the type layer forked is also this document's own reading of where `CJ-IDL` confers, R-12-013 conferring on the mapping and stating no ground for it.
  * **Twenty-one constructor rows, and every deletion cites.** Recursion and every unbounded former go on R-12-012 with R-05-143, open variants and accept-and-ignore cases on R-12-012 with R-05-051b, `future` and `stream` on having no admitted transport, `map` on having no declared key order and so nothing for a canonicity theorem to quantify over, and the upstream's `@since`/`@unstable`/`@deprecated` gates on a frozen profile having one version. Nothing is deleted on taste, and the set is closed by this document because R-12-012 restricts without enumerating.
  * **The mapping takes the stronger canonicity reading rather than waiting for the register to decide it.** Whether an IDL encoding is identity-bearing is undecided: R-13-003 puts the interface descriptor inside the content-addressed manifest, and R-05-051c's enumerated site list does not name a live call. So the encoding is authored to R-05-051b's no-slack rule whole and is **packed**, alignment being the destination buffer's rather than the wire's, because a copy-once parser writes its fixed buffer whole (R-05-124) and interior padding would be exactly the free padding that rule forbids. One length form per bound, one presence encoding, one discriminant width per case count, a declared zero fill checked rather than ignored, no reserved bit and no reserved field. The arm not taken buys encoding slack on the call path and a second audit at every site that later becomes identity-bearing.
  * **The Coq subset is the largest single act the document takes, and it is recorded as a decision rather than presented as a reading.** R-12-010's criterion has the admission checker verify each Tier-1 proof is stated against *the matching skeleton* and R-18-037 wants conformance among the generated client, server, parsers and skeleton; no entry says what a skeleton contains. The document fixes six contents and no seventh, gives every admitted constructor a Gallina image so no admitted declaration has a part the generator cannot state, and refuses a skeleton carrying a `Parameter` or an `Axiom`.
  * **What it does not carry is stated as a boundary.** No kernel ABI, R-07-031b closing that enumeration where the gate audits it and R-12-013 making the kernel not an IDL endpoint, cited rather than restated; no value of any composition magnitude, every one a field; no admission-checker behaviour, R-12-010 and R-18-037 both naming a checker no artifact here is; and no marshalling, no verified parser, and no systems-language binding. The ring schema is **not** among them: R-12-091's own criterion puts it inside this mapping rather than beside it, and it is carried at §4.3.
  * **Twenty-one judgments the register does not fix are collected in one declared-parameters table with a ground and a way to be wrong**, in the shape [freeze-measurement-contract.md](freeze-measurement-contract.md)'s §8 uses. That table is the discipline against the deepest hazard here: taking one inside a sentence of §2 or §4 would put a decision in an artifact the review gate does not audit, which is what six of the rows repair, the label's wire consequence, the byte order, the width ladder, the short-variant fill, the declaration's identity and where a value outside R-12-092's enumeration rides each having been taken in a requirement before they were declared. The sixth was found at the review gate rather than at the authoring, which is the hazard met once from inside the table's own section.
  * **What is built against the profile is one declaration and one generator, and the header says which.** [interfaces/ring-reference.json](../interfaces/ring-reference.json) is the one interface declaration and [run.py ring](../tools/vos/cli/ring.py) compiles it to the §4.3.6 interface artifact; no marshalling code, no verified parser, no systems-language binding and no admission checker exists, so most of what §4 and §6 oblige is a specification rather than a file. The two limits IDL-068 states bear on this cell unchanged: what is emitted is Gallina rather than systems-language bindings, and the admission checker R-12-010 and R-18-037 both name does not exist here.
  * **`CJ-IDL`, row 3 of the crown-jewel inventory, reads `not authored` and is ready to flip at §4.** Flipping it is a crown-jewels edit, which is Tier A for the author and re-enters R-05-150's review; this item reports the row and does not take it. No register, profile, absence-contract or coverage-matrix edit either, so the co-read ledger is unmoved.
  * **Four claims this document made about itself were false of the tree that carried them, and the repair is above rather than beside them.** §9 said nothing else in this repository states a wire encoding for the IDL, where [the ring emitter](../tools/vos/cli/ring.py) states one and K-89 holds the artifact against that emitter rather than against §4.2; that section now says so, and the binding it needs stays owed rather than repaired by a rule this document has no standing to invent. §9's *every enumeration is cited rather than restated* was false of IDL-051 and of IDL-053: the first no longer names R-12-091's four header words and its criterion no longer fixes their count, and the second cannot stop naming R-12-092's six member kinds, a mapping that assigns an encoding row per member having to name the kinds, so §9 states that one restatement and what holds it. §7's *no admission-checker behaviour* was contradicted by IDL-046 and by §8's own row. IDL-055 dropped R-12-093's teardown exception, which under this document's header made it defective against the register.
  * Net change: the new [idl-profile.md](idl-profile.md) at 529 lines, of which M6.4's §4.3 is 101 from its heading through its last requirement's trace, plus 19 lines in [THIRD-PARTY.md](../THIRD-PARTY.md) and 11 lines added against 7 removed in [vos/findings.py](../tools/vos/findings.py).
  * The actual is agent-session wall clock rather than the attended figure the thirty completed items were priced in: it sums one reconnaissance pass, the two licence reads and their confirmations, the authoring, three adversarial readings, the repair they carried, and the gates. It does not carry the review gate's own repair pass, which is priced at M6.4 where the artifact it also repaired lives.
  * Twenty-four findings, nineteen of them owed acts reported rather than closed: seventeen are register gaps, and the other two are a coverage cell this item leaves stale and the crown-jewel row it reports ready and does not flip.
    * **The component model states its terms through a one-line pointer and the forge reports its licence as `NOASSERTION`.** A reading that stopped at the forge would have carried no instrument at all, and one that stopped at the announcement would have missed that the pointer defers to subdirectory licences; the tree carries none, so the deferral resolves.
    * **Fuchsia's own `LICENSE` is `BSD-2-Clause`, not the three-clause instrument the common characterization carries.** There is no *Neither the name of* clause. Two independent renderings agree.
    * **No register entry pins this document or names its version.** R-05-135a pins the language document by name and R-05-135b makes a bump a review-gate event; R-12-010 says fork-and-frozen and names no artifact, so an amendment to the constructor set or to the wire-format mapping is not a register edit and the gate never reads it. This is F-190a's shape met a second time, on a document nothing pins rather than on enumerations a pinned document holds.
    * **The start-from is unnamed in the register.** Only the specification's §12 prose and [inspirations.md](inspirations.md) say what the fork is a fork of, and neither is the audited artifact, so naming it is a maturity claim under R-18-001a with no register owner.
    * **Fork-and-frozen has no freeze act here.** R-15-014a gives the ISA profile a two-act freeze with a closed nine-item delta and an instrument per row; no entry says when this profile freezes, what its delta is, or what a post-freeze change costs beyond R-18-034's general amendment.
    * **No entry closes the type-constructor set.** R-12-012 restricts without enumerating what constructors exist, so §2's table is closed by the document and by nothing the gate audits. R-07-027a's object-inventory closure at M4.2b is the precedent for the act that would close it.
    * **Whether a floating-point type is admitted is unstated, and it bears on canonicity rather than on taste.** NaN payloads and signed zero give one value several encodings, which R-05-051b forbids of any descriptor on an identity-bearing path.
    * **`string` has no admitted encoding and no statement of what its bound counts.** R-12-012 requires the bound and says nothing about bytes against code points, which encoding is admitted, or whether an ill-formed sequence is a decode failure, where UTF-8's over-long encodings are exactly R-05-051b's one-admissible-form case.
    * **Which result type carries the relevance grade is unstated.** R-05-098's closed list names IDL call outcomes as one member and R-12-093 admits a per-operation closed refinement variant beside the common status set; whether each refinement is graded at its own definition in R-18-016's sense or inherits the common set's grade is undecided.
    * **The grade has no stated wire consequence.** R-18-016 places it at the definition and R-05-097 makes it a typing property, and nothing says whether it is carried on the wire, so a mapping that encodes it and one that does not are both admissible. A grade on the wire is a field R-05-051b would then have to admit no slack in.
    * **The flow-label lattice has an owner and no enumeration, and the narrower claim is the true one.** R-07-025 fixes the confidentiality-label lattice at build with the composed topology, R-08-024 makes the fixed graph fix the lattice and the declassification points, and R-07-026 forbids either sanctioned transfer from adding a label, so the lattice is owned, it is composition-time, and the wire-or-composition question R-12-011 leaves open is answered by those three. What no entry states is its **membership**: no label set, no order, no join, and `CJ-NI`'s security policy model, row 2 of the inventory, is not authored, so IDL-041's predicates stay stated over an arbitrary lattice.
    * **Whether the IDL's own wire format is a member of the R-05-042 wire-format inventory is unstated.** R-12-024f puts translator content formats in it and R-05-046 makes each descriptor its own crown jewel; the mapping is row 3 under `CJ-IDL` rather than row 10 under `CJ-FORMAT`, and whether its descriptor is also a row-10 member, and so separately conferred, is undecided.
    * **Whether the mapping is on an identity-bearing path, and so owes an R-05-051a canonicity theorem, is undecided.** R-13-003 puts the interface descriptor inside the content-addressed manifest, which makes that descriptor's encoding look identity-bearing, while R-05-051c's enumerated site list names the pack reader, the typed manifest, the content address and the deterministic-reuse key and does not name a live IDL call.
    * **The receiver-validation declaration has no vocabulary.** R-12-012a requires the IDL to declare which received values carry the index, length, offset or selector obligation and says nothing about whether that is a per-field marker, a per-operation clause, or a type of its own.
    * **One constructor has two readings and no entry says whether that is one constructor or two.** R-12-010 maps resources to capabilities and R-12-006 with R-12-007 admit only session-table indices on the data plane, so `resource` carries a control-plane reading and a data-plane reading with nothing deciding whether they are one former.
    * **Nothing bounds the size of a declaration.** R-05-143 bounds every recursive former and R-12-012 bounds every list and string, both being bounds on values; the number of operations, types, fields, or worlds a declaration carries is not a runtime value and no entry bounds it.
    * **The Coq subset has no stated boundary.** R-12-010's criterion is that the admission checker verifies each Tier-1 proof is stated against the matching skeleton and R-18-037 requires conformance among four generated artifacts, and no entry says what a skeleton contains, so what a Tier-1 proof is stated against is undecided by the register.
    * **Two coverage cells read as though the profile existed.** `B-03` by `P-7` reads mode `proved` over R-12-010 and R-12-013a and `B-03` by `P-6` reads `admission-rejected` over R-12-012 and R-12-013, and the profile those four requirements fix did not exist when either mode was written. Authoring it makes neither proved: no proof, no checker and no declaration follows from a specification act.
    * **`CJ-IDL`'s row 3 is ready to flip and is not flipped here.** The status column reads `not authored`, §4 is the mapping that row names, and the flip is a crown-jewels edit whose reading is R-05-150's rather than this lane's. Until it is taken the tree states both halves of a contradiction, K-11 holding the row's id and K-25 only the status string's class, so nothing reads the disagreement.
    * **No entry gives an interface declaration an identity that moves when the declaration moves.** R-12-010's criterion has the admission checker verify each Tier-1 proof is stated against *the matching skeleton* and names nothing a match is decided on; a declaration name plus a profile version does not move when a declaration is edited in place, so a proof stated against the superseded skeleton would still match. The profile fixes a content address at IDL-036 as a declared parameter, which is a decision one document takes and no entry audits.
    * **The wire mapping's encoding shape has no register owner at three places.** The byte order of multi-byte scalars, the width ladder every length, count and discriminant is drawn from, and what a descriptor variant shorter than its declared slot leaves in it are each free: R-05-051b requires that one form be admissible and decides which at none of the three, and R-12-091 fixes the slot size without saying what fills it. Three declared parameters exist because of this.
    * **An encoding table is not reachable from a mapping's obligations unless something states where each declared value rides.** Every obligation of §4.2 was stated over the descriptor, which R-12-092 restricts to scalars, buffer references, a tag, a deadline and a flag set, so four rows of the encoding table encoded nothing any obligation placed and an operation taking a record or a string had no stated place on the wire. IDL-023a is the joining rule; the lesson is that a mapping owes a placement rule beside its encoding rows, and that a table whose rows nothing reaches reads as complete.
    * **K-82's count-word vocabulary stopped at twenty where the plan's own figure convention spells word forms to ninety-nine.** A note declaring more than twenty findings matched no block, so the rule read the note as recording none and failed on the disagreement rather than on the count, which is fail-loud and still points at the wrong side. The table is now [vos/figures.py](../tools/vos/figures.py)'s own word form rather than a second list, so the reading stops where the convention stops; no note before this one had crossed the boundary.
    * **The convention for an identifier inserted between two others and the permanence of identifiers collide once an out-of-order block has landed, and permanence holds.** §4.3's obligations are numbered IDL-050 through IDL-068 and sit between IDL-028 and IDL-029, where the house rule wanted IDL-028a onward; renumbering them would break the stronger rule, which this document restates in its own normative form and which the register's preamble states unconditionally. The repair is at the next insertion rather than at this one, and §4.3 says so in place.
  * Exit evidence, re-measured at the repair pass rather than carried from the landing: the three host gates green, `check.py` reporting every derived fact in agreement with its artifact across all **88** registered rules with the document tracked, the selftest at **100 mutants, all killed**, the repair path holding and the registry covered, and both type checkers clean at ty 0.0.75 and ruff 0.16.5; the tools' own tests green at **330 cases over 37 modules**, which this item owes because it changed what `vos/findings.py` reads; **K-82** reporting the register total at **388** findings across **70** items, of which this note's 24 are one block; **K-89** reporting the §4.3.6 artifact byte-identical to what `run.py ring emit` writes from the declaration and the **4** register entries it reads; and the two licence readings taken by fetch at each upstream's own `LICENSE` and dated, the Fuchsia one on two independent renderings and the component model's confirmed against its tree's own licence-file inventory. No guest run is owed for the profile itself: no model, RTL, oracle or corpus artifact is touched, and the proof and seeded figures the repair moved are M6.4's, where the artifact lives. No register, ISA-profile, crown-jewel, absence-contract or coverage-matrix edit, so the co-read ledger is unmoved; the repair does re-open R-05-150's reading of §7, §9 and IDL-051 and IDL-055, which is what this item's own Tier A landing already puts there.
  * Landed: Tier A.

#### M6.1a · The supervision tree as a Gallina state machine

  * The Gallina reference §8 owes is [SupervisionTree.v](../proofs/SupervisionTree.v), in the idiom the two landed Gallina items set. **Three closed inductives and no fourth**, one per enumeration the register itself closes: R-12-087's eight detectors and its ten actions, and R-12-089's five criticality classes, each count a silent conversion rather than a sentence. Everything a composition fixes is a field: the roster, the start order, the manifest, the retired set, the per-detector assertion and clear thresholds, the dwell, the intervention window, the backoff schedule and its ceiling, the boot bound, the detector-to-action map, the class ladders and the declared victim. A unit is a finite index below the roster count rather than an abstract carrier, which is the Rupicola subset kept as a discipline and not claimed as a result.
  * **The bring-up half reads the start order as a precedence over the manifest's own edges**, so starting a unit before its grantee is refused, and beside it the roster obligations that the order alone does not carry: every member started exactly once, and nothing named outside the signed roster. The loader is obliged to return the signed composition whatever it observes, refuted by one that drops the roster under pool pressure.
  * **The re-grant half states R-12-074 and R-10-037 apart, because read as one equality they contradict.** One says exactly the edges the manifest fixed; the other says authority is re-derived at the manifest *and the current revocation epoch*, so no restore resurrects what a revocation retired. The difference is machine-checked rather than argued: the construction that faithfully rebuilds the manifest mints nothing and resurrects a retired edge, so the two clauses are not one clause stated twice.
  * **Detection and reaction are stated as properties an alternative construction breaks rather than as absences.** A detector reads its own signal alone, refuted by one that scans every signal; an action does not vary with the observation, refuted by one that terminates past a threshold; a victim stays inside one criticality class, refuted by a selector that reaches the non-sacrificable unit under pressure; a bounded schedule bounds every attempt including past its last step, refuted by a doubling one that crosses at step four; admission respects the dwell and the window, refuted once per conjunct so neither is dead; and a reaction is a function of the declared state, refuted by a supervisor that answers differently at two states carrying one declared record.
  * **Twenty weakenings are generated over the start order rather than authored**, four transpositions, five deletions, five proper suffixes and six duplicate starts, every one refused as one conversion and again per family and again as a theorem quantified over the index. Each transposition is computed to break **exactly one** manifest edge and the specification to break none, which is the check that no conjunct is dead. Every refuting construction is also shown to satisfy the obligations it does not break, so what refuses it is the named defect and not its shape.
  * **Ten survivors on the first seeded run, every one real, and each closed by adding the statement whose absence it exposed.** Reading `precedes` at an absent unit was unreachable because the occurrence check refused a deletion first; four threshold comparisons had no signal sitting *on* the boundary; the stranger witness's index was unconstrained; and four steps of the doubling schedule never crossed the ceiling. Narrowing the population would have hidden all ten. The zero-width hysteresis band one of them exposed is now a stated theorem and a reported register gap.
  * **The gate's green line means compiled, axiom-free, non-vacuous and enumerated, and it does not mean verified.** Nothing here is lowered, run on either emulator, or a Lustre node; no timing, determinism or causality property is claimed and M6.1b owns the lowering. It models no memory, no unit lifecycle, no object class.
  * Net change: 1,905 lines added and none removed in one file, which is new.

#### M6.2a · The composition-time admission path

  * The CHERI-TAL type-checker M6.2 owes at composition time is [AdmissionPath.v](../proofs/AdmissionPath.v), in the idiom the landed Gallina items set. **The packages it checks are the golden-model components themselves**, which is this cell's own first clause: `golden_roster` names §§2 through 8, the Root-of-Trust firmware, the M-mode firmware, the kernel, the crypto core, the object system and its transactor, the filesystem, and the init system's supervision tree. §9's two checkers are named and are not roster members, R-06-014 making them exactly the admitters no admission certificate covers. **The derivations are thin**, which is the cell's second clause: a derivation here is a list of discharge records and carries no proof term, and `Terse` is the carrier whose every value reads as a certificate with no record at all.
  * **Four closed enumerations are the register's and two are inherited through a pin.** R-05-029's obligation rows, R-05-036's three checker moves, the facets that entry's criterion splits those rows into, and R-13-011's three tiers are the register's, each count a silent conversion. The typed assembly language's seven judgment forms and TAL-074's six checker phases arrive through R-05-135a's pin, which is a gap reported rather than closed. Everything a composition fixes is a field: the per-tier required sets past Tier 2's, the four admitted versions and the composer's identity.
  * **Tier 2's requirement is R-13-012's six rows and not a row more.** That entry names ABI and type well-formedness, no runtime codegen, temporal safety, definite initialization, data-race freedom and control-flow integrity; R-05-036 and R-05-038 are what make *temporal safety* the memory-safety row's move-II half, and R-05-029's own sentence is what makes control-flow integrity both of its halves. Six rows, seven facets. The reading that requires the whole memory-safety row is a bar stronger than the register's and is refuted by a machine that refuses what R-13-012 admits.
  * **The discharge kind is the axis the register fixes exactly.** R-05-029 has a derivation carry an attribute, citation or deletion-check for each obligation and R-05-037, R-05-038 and R-05-039 assign one to each facet, so a record naming the right facet and making the wrong move discharges nothing. The checker that reads the facet and ignores the move is refuted, and the generated misrouting family refuses exactly the facets R-05-037 routes through a citation. The judgment form is the axis no entry fixes, and that freeness is machine-checked rather than assumed.
  * **The order is TAL-074's and rejecting at the first failure has content at every adjacent pair.** Six phases, the version comparison in the first, the three move phases walked as deletions then citations then attributes. A checker that walks a tier's required set in the order its machine declares it fails closed, is complete, satisfies all eight refusal clauses, and still names a later phase where an earlier one is open. The three phases before them carry three clauses of their own, one per adjacent pair, each refuted by a checker that hoists the later phase above the earlier one and each of those three accepting exactly where the specification accepts; the pair TAL-074 itself leaves open, phase 0's commitment recomputation against its version comparison, is decided on TAL-008's *rejects before proceeding* and is refutable rather than asserted.

  * Class X and re-priced at M4.1b: the composition tooling [inspirations.md](inspirations.md) pointed at the CHERI-seL4 release for is authored here instead, that release's kernel half being GPL-2.0-only and its CHERI work contributed under the same tag.

Four of the five step two reaches carry a statement in the file and R-13-001 carries none, being the ordinary install path M6.2a states in [AdmissionPath.v](../proofs/AdmissionPath.v), and the header names it with what it is owed by rather than letting a derived scope carry an id nothing below is stated over. *Step three* is the judgment, and it reaches five more entries by a noun one of the four uses and does not itself define: R-12-024c's "pools" reaches R-08-046 and R-08-047, its "bounded-ring" reaches R-12-005, its acceptance clause's "confidentiality domain" reaches R-08-021, and R-12-024b's "routing" reaches R-12-024e. **A reviewer disagrees with step three**; steps one and two resolve against the register's own text.

Each carries its sentence beside its count and is quantified over rather than decorative, so dropping a constructor drops an obligation. **The same criterion is what leaves two open**: the media template's stage set and the intent variants are owed by entries that name no member, so each is a field and a reported gap.
  * **Well-formedness is twelve conjuncts held as a list, and the composer's filter is `all_of` over them.** That is what makes a weakening generated rather than authored on both sides: `spoiled_at k` is the composed edge with one field set to the value on conjunct k's own boundary, and `compose_without k` is the specification with conjunct k dropped from its own filter, so the twelve are decided twice, once from the edge and once from the composer. Each spoiled edge is computed to break **exactly one** of the twelve and the composed edge to break none; each dropped conjunct admits exactly one more edge than the specification.

Three theorems apiece rather than one, all over an arbitrary machine and roster: the discovering composer executes no package code and emits a closed graph, the executing one composes from the descriptors alone and emits a closed graph, and the trusting one does both. **A refuter shown only to be well formed isolates nothing**, the reader being left unable to say which of several defects the refutation turned on.

Both are machine-checked as edge counts and as edges-into-a-node, which is M6.1a's `the_regrant_extent_is_observable` method at a different subject, and **both are also shown to emit the node set the recomposition emits**, so what refutes each is the edge list and never a different roster.

Where the register leaves two disciplines open, a first-match and a last-match selector are both exhibited, each proved to keep every obligation the other keeps, with the difference machine-checked on a graph carrying two edges that match one intent.

The obligation is stated unconditionally at the conjunct and nothing about it is owed. **A gap is a gap only once every entry that could decide it has been read**, and the cost of getting that wrong is a register act with nothing to do.

The three-step form above separates them, which costs one sentence and makes the audit handle work.

The repair is two theorems and no new construction, and the general form is that a refuter's keeps-set is part of the refutation rather than commentary on it.
    * **Every figure in a note is measured at the revision the note ships, and a population figure taken before the last statement landed is the one that survives an edit unnoticed.** No checker rule reads a seeded-population figure, the exit evidence and the findings block state it twice, and the two can disagree two bullets apart with every gate green. What holds it is that the figure is re-measured at the gate rather than carried, which is the discipline the exit-evidence line already states and which a `Landed:` line does not enforce.

Every mutant of this file is refused by the prover and none by the vectors, so it never reaches the vector stage the enumerative harness pays for, and the closure is very nearly the whole per-mutant cost: the unmutated file compiles in **0.9 s** and the whole population runs at `--jobs 8` in **2 min 25 s**. That is the shape to price the next Gallina item at, and the qualification that travels with it is that the saving is this large only where the prover does the killing; on a file whose mutants reach the vectors it is a few per cent and the vector computation is the cost.
  * **Paying for a population and recording it are two acts, and the later reader has only the second.** The evidence is the file a run wrote, not the run: verdicts that went to a transcript are gone with the window, and the cell is then left carrying either a count nobody can check or an owed act. Two things hold it now and they are different things. The run writes to a file stamped with the revision it ran at, which is this item's own discipline; and the run states on its closing line what fraction of the population it covered, which is the tool's, so a narrowed run can no longer be quoted as a whole one by a reader who has only that line.

### M6.4 · Author the ring-contract schema and generate the reference bindings

  * The common ring schema and lifecycle R-12-091 through R-12-101 state are authored **into** [the typed IDL profile](idl-profile.md), at its §4.3, which is where R-12-091's own criterion puts them: content of the wire-format mapping rather than a second normative ring-semantics artifact beside it. M6.0b landed that mapping in the same lane and this cell is its first content.
  * **The schema is authored and the artifact is generated, which is this plan's own rule and not a style.** [interfaces/ring-reference.json](../interfaces/ring-reference.json) is the one authored owner of everything a composition fixes: the ring constants, the encoding widths, the closed operation set, each operation's scalars, buffer references, labels, cancellation declaration, and the per-variant record R-12-101 requires. [run.py ring](../tools/vos/cli/ring.py) compiles it to [proofs/RingContract.v](../proofs/RingContract.v), and **K-89** holds the tracked artifact byte-identical to what that command writes. Nothing is transcribed beside anything.
  * **The register is the artifact's second owner, and that is the part worth the rule.** R-12-093's status set and R-12-094's lifecycle states are read out of those entries' own lines, R-12-095's full-ring result and R-12-097's cancellation answers beside them, so the artifact carries four enumerations the register owns and no copy of any of them exists in the emitter or in the declaration. A status added, a state reordered, or a token typed into the artifact by hand is one finding each at the gate the edit lands at. The emitter reads them through [vos/register.py](../tools/vos/register.py)'s parse rather than a second one, so the generator adds no reader for a document the checker already reads.
  * **The campaign is generated from the artifact's own constants**, which is R-18-037's criterion read fail-closed: a declaration whose constants break an obligation does not compile. The obligations land over one lemma, each anchored on an entry or on a requirement of the profile rather than invented. The two width rules, IDL-023's ladder pinned at each of its own boundaries and the flag set spending its declared width exactly (WF-10); the descriptor's and the completion's encoded sizes against their declared slots, and both slots' alignment (R-12-091, and §4.2's packed rule); the index span against its own declared width and the capacity dividing it; fill to capacity and the refusal one past it (R-12-095); completion capacity against the maximum simultaneously accepted; the batch bound, the drain inside it, and the declared batch and segment maxima each attained by a named operation (R-12-098, R-12-100); notifications coalesced to one (R-12-096); segments bounded and covering the declared payload exactly (R-12-100); the activation cost inside the slot budget and the cancellation interval inside the declared time to terminal completion (R-12-101, R-12-097); cancellability being the declaration and nothing else, a non-cancellable operation declaring no cancellation at all, and the commit point among the declared points (R-12-097); labels inside the declared lattice (R-12-011); the empty-validation case as a claim rather than a default (R-12-012a); the lifecycle advancing monotonically with one terminal state, and the malformed step skipping forward without acquiring authority (R-12-094); a stale generation and a duplicate live identifier each refused and a fresh unique request accepted (R-12-092, R-12-099); that no consumer's sleep rule leaves published work behind, refuted of a consumer that skips the recheck (R-12-096); and the two answers cancellation's race fixes, a target past its commit point too late and a non-cancellable operation never live (R-12-097).
  * **The reference bindings are Gallina, which is the cell's own first limit rather than a shortfall against it.** §0 puts base components the specification assigns to safe Rust in Gallina for the reference, and no purecap backend exists, so what the compiler emits is the interface artifact in the reference language: the descriptor and completion records, the submit, accept, cancel and notification functions a client and a server are stated over, and the constants both read. Systems-language client and server bindings wait on M1.2.
  * **The second limit is the admission checker, and it is unmoved.** R-12-010's criterion and R-18-037's campaign each name it; M6.2's composition-time half is landed and its on-device half is not, so the artifact this item produces has an admission rule to satisfy and no checker to be admitted by. Neither limit is repaired by narrowing the schema, and the profile states both at IDL-068 rather than in this cell alone.
  * **The gate's green line means emitted, compiled, axiom-free and held, and it does not mean verified.** No ring exists, no service rides one, and no canonical SPSC, lost-wakeup or typestate proof is written; R-18-037 names those and this artifact claims none of them. What the campaign decides is that the declared composition satisfies the obligations the register states over it, which is admission arithmetic and not a proof about an implementation.
  * **The seeded population is what says whether those obligations constrain the declaration, and its first run said they did not.** Fifteen of twenty-four sampled mutants survived, every one an incremented composition constant the campaign's inequalities absorbed as slack. Each was closed by adding the statement its absence exposed rather than by narrowing the population, and the statements are one discipline: **a composition declares its margin instead of leaving it to be computed**, so a bound over a declared quantity becomes an equality against a declared slack and a weakening moves a figure rather than eating headroom. **Three obligations are still plain inequalities and still absorb an incremented constant**, and each is a relation between two constants the declaration already fixes with no third standing as the margin: completion capacity against the maximum simultaneously accepted, the batch against the capacity, and an operation's commit point among its declared points. Closing them is the same act at three more places, a declared headroom in the declaration rather than a statement in the artifact, and it is not taken here. The declared fill against the descriptor slot and the completion slot, the declared activation slack against the slot budget, the declared payload slack against the segments, and the declared cancellation slack against the time to terminal completion are that device at four places; the index span pinned to its own declared width and the capacity required to divide it are R-12-091's modulo interpretation made arithmetic; the width rule pinned at each of its own boundaries is IDL-023's one admissible form, and the flag set's declared width spent exactly by its flags and its declared spare bits is WF-10's; the lifecycle successor stated as *rank plus one* rather than *rank greater* is R-12-094's sequence read as a sequence; and a fifth reference operation was added because no declared operation carried a marked scalar without a buffer reference, so the disjunction deciding the receiver-validation obligation was untested by the declaration rather than unstated by the profile.
  * **The review gate found four claims no rule reads, and all four are repaired here.** Each was true of nothing and green everywhere, which is what a gate over derived facts cannot reach. The flag set's width is now declared where WF-10 says it lives and the emitter's second ladder is gone, so the one ladder the artifact carries is IDL-023's and the rung §8 refuses is not in it; a new obligation spends the declared width exactly, so a flag set the declared width no longer holds fails to compile rather than being absorbed. The malformed step's two ends and the unstarted target are read out of R-12-094's and R-12-097's own sentences and checked against the chain, so a state inserted between them moves the artifact, which is what IDL-056's Accept says of it; today's register emits the same bytes. §9's absence claim is repaired at the profile, where M6.0b's cell records it. And **K-89**'s third fail-closed place is one now rather than a crash, the emitter naming every key it reads where the reading is refused and the rule catching what no guard reaches, with [tools/tests/test_ring.py](../tools/tests/test_ring.py) the subject module the one-per-subject convention was owed and the thing that decides the repair rather than asserting it.
  * Net change over the files this item owns outright: 1,968 lines added and 1 removed across nine, of which 643 are the generated artifact, 861 the compiler, 157 the declaration, 104 the rule and 183 the rule's test module; the profile's §4.3 is 101 more, inside a document M6.0b owns.
  * Fourteen findings, eight of them owed acts: seven are register gaps reported rather than closed, and the eighth is a binding owed in the tools. Seven were raised by the review gate's own reading of this landing rather than by the authoring, and four of those are closed by the repair the cell records above.
    * **R-12-091 names an index width and no entry says what it counts.** The mapping needs a unit before it can hold a capacity against it, and §4.2's other widths are byte widths, so the declaration counts bytes; nothing in the register decides that.
    * **R-12-091's constant list is not the set a ring declaration must fix.** This declaration fixes fourteen ring constants where that entry names six. Four of the other eight are named by some other entry and by none as a ring constant: the maximum simultaneously accepted requests and the completion capacity (R-12-095), the maximum segment count (R-12-100, and R-12-101 for the per-variant bound over it), and the slot budget the joint proof is taken against (R-12-101). The remaining four are named by no entry at all: a segment's maximum size, which R-12-100 does not fix where it fixes a maximum segment count, the index span, the completion slot's size, and its declared fill.
    * **Nothing joins R-12-094's six states to R-12-097's cancellation answers.** That entry decides the answers by where a target stands against its declared points and never says which of the six lifecycle states are live to cancel, so the mapping of its four situations onto the six is the profile's rather than the register's.
    * **No entry says which of R-12-093's eight statuses are refusals.** R-12-092's criterion has a malformed descriptor produce *one of the defined refusal completions* that closed set names, and the set is stated as one vocabulary with no refusal subset marked, so the checker reading a refusal has nothing enumerated to read.
    * **R-12-101 names the terms of the joint bound and no inequality.** It requires the composition to prove that capacity, batch size, polling cadence, slot budget and device latency jointly prevent overwrite and meet the declared progress bound, which is five quantities and no relation, so the arithmetic the campaign decides is this document's and the register audits none of it.
    * **The guest lane failed mid-item and the failure is the machine's rather than the tree's.** WSL began refusing every launch with `Wsl/Service/E_UNEXPECTED` after the proof gate had run green, recovering only in brief windows, so the seeded population was taken as a sample across that failure rather than as one run. The proof gate's figures were measured before it, on the bytes `run.py ring check` holds the tracked artifact to.
    * **The artifact shipped a width rung the profile's own declared-parameter table says this profile does not have.** §8 states the ladder as one, two or four bytes with no eight-byte rung and names what would add one, a declaration needing a bound at or above 2^32, which does not exist; a second ladder in the emitter ended in that rung anyway, under a comment attributing both to IDL-023, and a shipped theorem machine-checked it. IDL-023 fixes no flag-set width at all: WF-10 makes a flags value a bit field of the **declared** width, and the declaration declared none, so the emitter derived one.
    * **A figure computed as the difference of two ordinal positions reads no order at all.** The malformed step's skip was `states.index(states[4]) - states.index(states[2])`, which is two for any chain of distinct states, under a docstring saying it came from the register's own order. It made IDL-056's Accept, *a state added or reordered there moves the artifact*, false of the added half, with **K-89** green throughout because that rule holds the artifact against the emitter.
    * **Nothing held the emitter's own statement of the wire encoding against §4.2, and I15 has since written the binding.** The emitter writes IDL-023's ladder as a Gallina literal and §4.2's packed rule as three encoded sizes; **K-89** holds what it writes against the emitter and the declaration, which is agreement with the artifact's owners and not with the mapping, so a row edited in the profile moves neither. This is the drift the rung walked through, and the binding was left owed here rather than repaired by a rule invented in the profile. **K-99** is that rule, recomputing the ladder from the rungs IDL-023 states in words and holding IDL-053's and `WF-11`'s members against the widths the two encoded sizes spend.
    * **A rule claiming three fail-closed places had two.** The checker caught the emitter's own refusal and nothing else, and [check.py](../tools/check.py) carries no `except` at all, so an owner shaped in a way no guard named ended the whole run. It was reachable: the key guard covered eight declaration keys and omitted the one `emit()` reads for the label lattice, and the arity guard read two states where the emitter indexed five.
    * **A seeded population figure is read by no rule, and this one was measured against a working copy rather than against the artifact that landed.** The cell said 191 where the tracked artifact yielded 215, a fifth of the population absent from the sentence describing it, with every gate green. It is re-measured at each repair now rather than carried.
    * **Where a value outside R-12-092's enumeration rides is undecided by the register.** That entry enumerates what a descriptor carries and says nothing about a value the enumeration does not admit or about how a delegated buffer's payload is encoded, so reading the enumeration as a placement rather than as a deletion is a decision; IDL-023a took it inside a §4.2 requirement before §8 carried it.

The generated record carries the field and §4.2 has no row for it, so the encoded size does not spend it and the field is inert until one of the two entries moves.
    * **What the sample does not reach is what the register states no relation over, which is not the same as what it does not state.** Each of the four survivors is one of four things and none is a defect in the statements: a flow label, which nothing can constrain while the lattice has no owner anywhere; the count of an operation's marked scalars, which nothing bounds above while the receiver-validation declaration has no vocabulary; the count of a cancellable operation's declared cancellation points, which R-12-097 requires to exist and bounds in no direction; and a derived count the emitter writes, which the prover cannot reach and **K-89** does, a hand-edited count no longer being what `run.py ring emit` writes. One site outside the sample is the sharper case and belongs here: `ring_session_generation` occurs once in the artifact, at its own definition, so no statement reads it, and R-12-091 **does** name a session generation among the constants a ring instance carries. The gap there is in the relation rather than in the enumeration, and no statement over the declaration alone can close it.
  * Exit evidence, re-measured at the repair pass rather than carried from the landing: the three host gates green with all **100 mutants killed**, the repair path holding and the registry covered at **88 rules**; both type checkers clean at ty 0.0.75 and ruff 0.16.5; the tools' own tests green at **330 cases over 37 modules**, of which [test_ring.py](../tools/tests/test_ring.py)'s **5** are this item's; the proof gate at **3,360 constants**, **33 of them this file's**, every one closed under the global context, with no `Require`, no `Compute` and no top-level axiom; `run.py ring check` reporting the tracked artifact byte-identical to what the emitter writes from the declaration and the **4** register entries it reads; and a seeded sample of **32 of the 207 mutants over nine operators, 28 killed, 4 surviving and none stillborn**, stillborn reported apart and being zero, every survivor real and each named above. **Both verdicts are the prover's alone and the sample is a one-oracle result**: [Vectors.v](../tools/quickchick/Vectors.v) requires the partition, executive, probe and endpoint sources and never this file, so the generated input side reaches none of it and every prover-accepted mutant survives the second oracle by construction, which is a measurement rather than a complaint. **The whole population is owed and is not run**: this sample alone was still running at **35 minutes** of wall clock against a guest carrying another lane's population, and the population is six and a half times it, so it is spread evenly across all nine operators rather than taken as a prefix of one. No `run.py evidence` sweep is owed: it measures the model build, the property harness, the profile sweep, the corpus, the devicetree and the reference, and this item touches none of them. No register, ISA-profile, crown-jewel, absence-contract or coverage-matrix edit, so the co-read ledger is unmoved; the repair re-opens R-05-150's reading of §4.3 at IDL-027, IDL-051, IDL-055 and IDL-060, which is where this item's own Tier A landing already puts it.
  * **The landing is Tier A rather than Tier B, and the rule it created does not change that.** **K-89** holds the artifact against its owners, which is the fourth mechanical condition met; what puts the reading at Tier A is the profile's §4.3, a run of numbered obligations that are new normative claims of a crown-jewel specification. A rule holding a generated file is not a substitute for reading what the file is generated to satisfy.
  * Landed: Tier A.

#### M6.5a · The copy-based service

  * Class X and re-priced at M4.1b for the same reason M6.3a is: the ring library that release supplied is authored here, so the SPSC, notification and capacity proofs stand over code this repository wrote rather than over maintained code it read.
  * The service is [CopyRingService.v](../proofs/CopyRingService.v), in the idiom the landed Gallina items set, and it is the first artifact here that **Requires a sibling**: [RingContract.v](../proofs/RingContract.v) is the generated interface artifact **K-89** holds byte-identical to its emitter, so it is the one owner of the declared constants, the closed status set, the six lifecycle states and the per-variant records this file is stated over. Every one of them is instantiated and none is transcribed, nothing declared here shadows a name that file exports, and the cost is one compile per run rather than one per mutant, nothing under [proofs/](../proofs/) Requiring this file in turn.
